# HyperMesh — official Help index (2022.3)

This generated catalog maps **2,298 index terms** to **2,438 official page references** (1,290 distinct page paths). It is a navigation/index layer, not a replacement copy of the Help pages.

Source: [https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/indexTerms.htm](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hm/indexTerms.htm) (HTTP 200; page title: Index Terms).

For task routing and version-selection rules, start at [Official Help catalog](../README.md). Search this file for the user-facing command, panel, concept, or API name, then open its official page. Entries may point to the same page; keep the source label and release attached when summarizing.
